- **annotate-ask-playwright**: INVOKE THIS SKILL when users ask questions about specific visual UI elements (e.g., "What is this button?", "What does this section do?", "Can you change this element's color?") or when they want to identify/discuss particular page components, and the agent lacks information to infer which part of the web page the user refers to. This skill lets users draw/circle elements instead of describing them. - **anomaly-detection-alerting**: 通过数据分析自动识别绩效管理中的异常模式和风险信号，在问题升级前及时预警相关管理者和HR。异常检测覆盖评估偏差、绩效波动、人才流失风险和流程合规性等多个维度，是绩效管理从事后反应转向事前预防的关键能力。 ([aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform](https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform/tree/HEAD/super-agent/industry-packs/industry-pack-human-resources/scopes/performance-management/skills/anomaly-detection-alerting) · ★27 · 2 copies)
- **anomaly-detection-alerting**: 本技能负责实时监控设备运行数据，通过规则引擎和异常模式检测识别设备故障、安全风险和异常行为，并按分级策略触发告警通知。这是保障智能家居安全运行的关键防线，需要在灵敏度（不漏报）和特异性（不误报）之间取得平衡。 ([aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform](https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform/tree/HEAD/super-agent/industry-packs/industry-pack-iot/scopes/smart-home-control/skills/anomaly-detection-alerting) · ★27 · 2 copies)
- **anomaly-detection-alerting**: 本技能用于实时监控销售数据中的异常模式，通过统计方法和业务规则的组合检测机制，在数据异常发生的第一时间生成分级预警，帮助管理层和相关团队快速识别风险并采取行动。该技能是销售管理"早期预警系统"的核心能力，其价值在于将被动发现问题转变为主动防控风险。 ([aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform](https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform/tree/HEAD/super-agent/industry-packs/industry-pack-sales-management/scopes/sales-analytics/skills/anomaly-detection-alerting) · ★27 · 2 copies)
- **anomaly-detection-analysis**: 本技能用于对企业内部数据源进行持续扫描和模式分析，识别可能指示合规违规的异常行为信号。采用基于规则的检测与统计异常检测相结合的方法，覆盖反商业贿赂、数据合规、反洗钱等多个合规领域的异常模式识别。 ([aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform](https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform/tree/HEAD/super-agent/industry-packs/industry-pack-legal-compliance/scopes/compliance-audit/skills/anomaly-detection-analysis) · ★27 · 2 copies)
- **anomaly-detection-analysis**: 本技能负责对巡检采集的原始数据进行深度异常检测，包括阈值判定、趋势恶化识别、跨层指标关联分析，并对异常项进行严重程度分级和根因初步推断。这是将原始指标数据转化为可行动洞察的关键分析环节。 ([aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform](https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform/tree/HEAD/super-agent/industry-packs/industry-pack-it-operations/scopes/daily-inspection-monitoring/skills/anomaly-detection-analysis) · ★27 · 2 copies)
